# Change Requests — process and register

Every change to BOFFO gets a number here. The register is the answer to *"what was asked, did
it ship, when, and where's the proof"*.

Raw requests as originally written live in [`change-requests/raw/`](change-requests/raw/).
The system as it currently stands is [`SYSTEM.md`](SYSTEM.md); the dated narrative of what
shipped is [`CHANGES.md`](CHANGES.md).

**Phases.** CR-001…CR-260 (8 June → 1 October 2026) are **Phase 1**, inventoried for end users and
billing in [`PHASE-1-FEATURES.md`](PHASE-1-FEATURES.md). Everything from **CR-261** onward is
**Phase 2** — file it under the Phase 2 heading at the end of this file and list it in
[`PHASE-2-FEATURES.md`](PHASE-2-FEATURES.md) when it ships.

---

## How to file a change request

Six fields. **CHANGE and WHERE are mandatory**; the rest sharpen the result.

```
CHANGE:    <the outcome, one line>
WHERE:     <screen / form / master>
WHY:       <the user goal — this sets how deep to build>
APPLY TO:  <"everywhere this pattern exists" | a list | "only here">
LIKE:      <an existing thing to match>
DONE WHEN: <the acceptance check>
```

`APPLY TO` is the field that matters most. The recurring failure in this project is a change
made in one place and left in its siblings.

## Standing rules — applied to every change without being asked

1. **Consistency sweep.** Grep the pattern across every sibling form, grid and master, and fix
   them together — at the root, not at one caller. `APPLY TO: everywhere` means exhaustively.
2. **Reuse, no one-offs.** Use the existing component, style or util. Never a bespoke duplicate.
3. **A new *rule* goes to memory AND gets retrofitted** in the same pass — to every existing
   screen, not just going forward.
4. **Verify by driving the flow.** A typecheck is not verification.

Any rule can be overridden per-request in one line (`APPLY TO: only here`).

## Statuses

| Status | Means |
|---|---|
| **Open** | Requested, not built. |
| **In progress** | Being built now. |
| **Done** | Built and verified. **Must cite a commit or `file:line`** — an uncited Done is not a Done. |
| **Deployed** | Done and live, with the date. |
| **Parked** | Deliberately not doing it now. Needs a reason and a date. |
| **Superseded** | Overtaken by a later decision. Names what replaced it. |

## Closing a change

A change is not finished until all five are true:

1. The code ships.
2. [`SYSTEM.md`](SYSTEM.md) reflects the new behaviour.
3. [`CHANGES.md`](CHANGES.md) has the dated entry.
4. The row here carries `Done`/`Deployed` **and its citation**.
5. [`DATA-FLOW.md`](DATA-FLOW.md) has the new write path or rule on the entity's row, and the rule
   was applied to **every** path in that row — not the one the request named.

---

# Register

Seeded 2026-08-29 from the raw request files that had accumulated at the repo root. Every item
below was checked against the code before it was given a status. Where the check was
inconclusive the status is **Open** — deliberately, because a register that guesses is worse
than one that admits it doesn't know.

Numbering is by source cluster, not by date raised — the raw files were undated.

## In flight

Work that has shipped to LIVE but is **not yet committed**. Tracked here so its state is
unambiguous if asked again.

|---|---|---|---|

color-coded SO chip card · mono items table with per-SO/per-item colors · container graphic

mapped onto the app theme tokens. The project's second file, `Plan Containerisation.dc.html`,
was not applied (out of scope for this CR).

## CR-129…139 · 2026-09-10 batch — plan stickiness, partial palletise, approvals & access

| CR | Change | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| 129 | **Bug (SO 27/032): palletization plan edits didn't stick.** Four root causes fixed: (a) `autoEnqueuePalletization` merged produced boxes back into a user-reduced Planning line — new `manual_edit` column (stamped by `/pal-plan` + `/update-pal-plan`, carried through every split) now PINS an item once its plan line is user-authored: no auto top-up, the remainder waits in the work list; (b) `/update-pal-plan` soft-deleted ALL lines and re-inserted them as `Planning`, resetting advanced lines and stripping `load_box`/`pallet_group` — it now replaces **Planning lines only**; (c) `PalPlanForm` edit mode sourced rows from the available-only work list (fully-palletised lines silently vanished on save) and clamped typing to produced-available ("type 450, get 400") — plan lines now merge back in, non-Planning boxes show as locked "+N palletised", and the clamp is the **SO ordered qty across all lines/plans** (user rule: split freely, never exceed the order; `assertPlanWithinOrdered` mirrors it server-side); (d) `PalletiseModal` let the container-plan prefill override a line's saved pallet — the saved pallet now wins. | **Built 2026-09-10** | `functions/data-ops/index.js` (`normalizePlanLines`, `assertPlanWithinOrdered`, `/update-pal-plan`, `autoEnqueuePalletization`); [`PalPlanForm.tsx`](../client/src/features/stages/PalPlanForm.tsx); [`PalletiseModal.tsx`](../client/src/features/stages/PalletiseModal.tsx); new `PalletizationPlanLine.manual_edit` column. |
| 130 | **Quote keeps ONE combined line per item** — planning 400+100 across pallets/containers no longer splits the quote line; the split lives only in the `container_plan` JSON. The quote planner's save now merges draft lines by item (qty summed, first rate kept) before `updateQuoteWithItems`. | **Built 2026-09-10** | [`PlanContainerisation.tsx`](../client/src/features/quotes/PlanContainerisation.tsx) quote `onSave`. |
| 131 | **Log production regardless of existing qty** — the Desired-qty clamp, covered-line read-only text, fully-covered SO picker filter and the hidden "Record New Production" menu item are gone (hint columns + signal dot unchanged); the server 409 cap (`pending+requested > ordered`) removed. | **Built 2026-09-10** | [`ProductionForm.tsx`](../client/src/features/stages/ProductionForm.tsx); [`OrderDetail.tsx`](../client/src/features/orders/OrderDetail.tsx); `index.js` `/production-record-lines` cap. |
| 132 | **Partial palletisation** — PalletiseModal takes a per-line Boxes qty (default full); `/pal-line-status` accepts `boxes` and SPLITS the line (slice transitions, remainder keeps its stage), so palletising part of a line never flips the rest to Ready for Loading. Same split pattern as `/pal-topup`. | **Built 2026-09-10** | [`PalletiseModal.tsx`](../client/src/features/stages/PalletiseModal.tsx); [`palPlansApi.ts`](../client/src/features/stages/palPlansApi.ts) `setPalLineStatus`; `index.js` `/pal-line-status`. |
| 133 | **Manual box weight** — Box Weight is editable on the Pallet form (Size value is the prefill; explicit size pick re-fills) and stays editable on the Item form; `propagateSizeSnapshots` overwrites a row's weight only when it still equals the OLD size value (or is blank), so a manual override survives Size edits. `/resync-size-snapshots` keeps full overwrite. | **Built 2026-09-10** | [`PalletForm.tsx`](../client/src/features/masters/PalletForm.tsx); `index.js` `propagateSizeSnapshots` + Size PATCH. |

Filed from: "Production Record first, then SO allocation — remove Record Production from the SO. Box Brand and Batch recorded at production time; redesign the production record screen, order-independent only. Manual override for a 100%-full container; multi-design mix-up in loading. Loading for multiple SOs of a single customer: select the customer, see all the design orders, initiate the loading — same container, same truck." The Customer/Sales Order comboboxes, the **Container Size** field (now captured in Assign Vehicle) and the separate plan-view/flat-view split are gone — one list, one state (`qty` per line). | 202 | **Batch pick; plan is a helper.** A design with several batches gets a design-level Load qty that spreads FIFO over its batch sub-rows (`spreadQty` → `allocateFifo`); each batch qty stays editable, so the user chooses the batches. Several designs / orders of the customer mix freely into the ONE container (= one truck). The SO's containerisation plan no longer drives the screen: a per-SO **Fill from plan** button prefills the next unsent container's quantities, and designs outside the plan stay loadable. `load_plan` lines always carry the batch. ## CR-205…207 · Security review fixes (2026-09-19)

Filed from: the `/security-review` of `feature/master-order-forms` — three validated findings, "fix these one by one, make no mistakes". Server-only; no screen changes.

| CR | Change | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| 205 | **Image upload is raster-only, and the public image route cannot run script.** `/upload/design-image` judges the file by its magic bytes (JPG, PNG, GIF, WebP, BMP, AVIF) — the client-sent name and type are not trusted; an SVG/HTML payload is refused with a 400, and the stored extension is corrected to match the bytes. `/public/design-image/:fileId` now answers with `nosniff` and a sandboxing CSP, so an SVG stored before this change still renders inside `<img>` but runs no script when opened as a page on the app origin. | **Done — NOT deployed**; `node --test functions/data-ops/lib/*.test.js` passes (11) | [`lib/sniffImage.js`](../functions/data-ops/lib/sniffImage.js) + test, `index.js:588` (headers), `index.js:821` (allowlist) |
| 206 | **Permission lookup ignores path case.** Express matches routes case-insensitively, the guard looked the first path segment up exact-case — `/DISPATCH/1` missed `ROUTE_PERM`, fell to the legacy `can_update` rule and still reached the handler. The lookup is now lower-cased. Table names stay exact-case (`assertTable` rejects any other spelling). | **Done — NOT deployed** | `lib/appauth.js:316` |
| 207 | **No state on a generic insert.** The generic `POST /:table` now refuses Quote and SalesOrder outright (they are born through `/quote-with-items`, `/so-with-items`, `/convert-quote`, where born status follows `canApprove`), and the status columns the generic PATCH already protected are protected on insert too — one shared list, `STATE_COLUMNS`, for both verbs. The only state an insert may carry is a Panel Order's initial `Received`. | **Done — NOT deployed**; guard self-check run | `index.js:5383` (`STATE_COLUMNS`, `assertNoStateWrite`), `index.js:5410` |

Not fixed here (review findings below the reporting bar, still worth a CR): raw `:rowid` in ZCQL on 14 business routes (`rowidParam` exists, those routes skip it); mutating routes missing from `ROUTE_PERM` (`update-so-with-items`, `production-*`, `allocate-stock`, …) and generic writes to `OperationLog` / `AppSetting`; the session token copy in `localStorage`.

# Phase 2 — from 2026-10-01 (CR-261 onward)

Phase 1 closed at CR-260 on 2026-10-01 ([`PHASE-1-FEATURES.md`](PHASE-1-FEATURES.md)). Every new
change request goes below this line and is added to [`PHASE-2-FEATURES.md`](PHASE-2-FEATURES.md)
when it ships.

## CR-261 · Container Master; Pallet Master is boxes only (2026-10-03)

| CR | Change | Status | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| 261 | **New Inventory master "Container Master"** (`/containers`, after Pallet Master): a container *format* = ONE Size + the pallet formats of that size with a count each (user: "the container will have multi pallet box accommodation abilities, but still fitting to 1 fix size only"). Flow: New → pick Size → tick pallets, type Pallets / Container per pallet → totals (Σ pallets, Σ boxes) and the name (`<size> · N pallets = M boxes`) are formula-owned. **Exactly one format per Size** (user decision): sizes that already have one leave the picker; server `NATURAL_KEY.ContainerFormat = "size"`. New Catalyst table `ContainerFormat` (LIVE id 69851000000313445: `name`, `size` FK→Size, `pallets_json` `[{pallet, count}]`, `total_pallets`, `total_boxes`, `remarks`, `deleted_at`) — ponytail: lines in a JSON column, no child table. Grid (Size · Pallets · Total Pallets · Total Boxes; Name/Created/Modified hidden), FormPage form, split detail with rail + Activity, Clone/Delete; module `items`. **Pallet Master is boxes only** (user: "Remove Per Container and keep Boxes only"): the Pallets / Container field and the whole Per Container (computed) section are gone; packing reads `32 boxes`, names `800x1600 - 32 boxes - Junglee`; Arrangement = Boxes / Pallet · Empty Pallet Weight · Loaded Pallet Weight ƒx. `pallets_per_container` + the `b_*` columns stay in the table unused. **Capacity readers moved to the Container Master, per SIZE**: `palPlansApi.palletCapacity` (loading / palletization fill %) = the size's `total_boxes`; Plan Containerisation boxes mode = the size's format (`no Container format for size … — add it in Container Master` when missing); a size without a format = capacity 0 = no fill %. Pallet pick per line stays (it still gives boxes per pallet). Legacy per-shipment `masters/Containers.tsx` moved to `/container-shipments` (still no nav entry). Seed script seeds one format for the ZZT size. | BUILT 2026-10-03 (tsc + build + `containerPack` / `newLoadingRows` self-checks pass; not browser-driven), DEPLOYED LIVE 2026-10-03 (functions + client), live drive + committed `dd54401` | `containerFormatsApi.ts`, `ContainerFormats.tsx`, `ContainerFormatForm.tsx`, `ContainerFormatFormPage.tsx`, `ContainerFormatDetail.tsx`, `PalletForm.tsx`, `palletsApi.ts`, `Pallets.tsx`, `PalletDetail.tsx`, `PalletFormPage.tsx`, `DetailBits.tsx`, `palPlansApi.ts`, `PlanContainerisation.tsx`, `App.tsx`, `functions/data-ops/index.js` (ALLOWED + NATURAL_KEY), `lib/appauth.js` (MODULE_NAV + TABLE_MODULE), `scripts/seed-flow.mjs` |

## CR-411…413 · Location at every stage — produced, palletized, ready for loading, panels (2026-10-24)

User asked: "how are we mapping the locations of Produced items, Ready for loading, loading for palletization and
every completed item? Give me places where items need a location, and how can we make it easy for users to assign
it when things are ready." Audit found: produced stock had a location only through the Record Output modal (four
other writers sent null → "▸ Unassigned"); palletized stock had no location at all; the Palletization / Loading Bay
purposes were labels that drove nothing. Decision kept from 2026-10-12: palletising / loading never write stock
transfers — a pallet's location is a **tag on the line**, not a movement.

| CR | Change | Status | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| 411 | **Every production writer records a location; warehouse purposes drive the default.** `defaultNodeFor(rows, purpose)` → `useWarehouses().primaryFor("Production" · "Palletization" · "Loading Bay")` = the first bay of the first warehouse carrying that purpose, else the primary's first location. The bulk Record Production sheet, the Complete Production dialog and the Excel import gain the same **Location** picker Record Output has (default = the Production bay); the inline grid record defaults silently (no room in a cell); Record Output's default switches from "primary" to the Production bay. WHERE: /prod sheet, Complete dialog, Import, grid inline, Record Output. DONE WHEN: nothing recorded from a production screen reads "▸ Unassigned". | **DEPLOYED LIVE** 2026-10-24 · commit pending | `warehousesApi.ts` (`defaultNodeFor`), `useWarehouses.ts` (`primaryFor`), `ProductionLogSheet.tsx`, `ProductionCompleteForm.tsx`, `ProductionImport.tsx`, `ProductionTable.tsx`, `RecordOutputForm.tsx`, `productionApi.ts` (`completeProduction.warehouse`) |
| 412 | **A pallet has a location from palletization until it enters a container.** New live column `PalletizationPlanLine.warehouse` (69851000000376694). Captured where the user already acts: the **Palletise dialog** gets ONE Location for every item (Start → Palletization bay, Complete → Loading Bay, both overridable) and `/pal-line-status` stamps it (`body.warehouse`, split slice included). Change later via **Set Location** — `SetLocationModal` moved to `common/` and reused from the boards' "+" menu (checked items ride along), the selection bar **Set Location (n)**, the /loading row menu (Ready for Loading only) and the plan detail More menu (every un-loaded line) — generic PATCH per line (`setPalLineLocation`). Splits keep the location (`/pal-topup` donor takes the target's; `writeLineIntoBox` remainder). Shown as a **Location** column on the packing sheet (`packingSheetColumns.v3`) and loading sheet (`loadingColumns.v4`; hidden while no warehouse exists), a Location group-by on /packing, the card meta line, the plan detail lines, Item ▸ Locations ▸ **On pallets at**, and a **Pallets** segment on Stock Locations (qty = boxes, row → the plan). A loaded line reads "—" (the container is the place). The reducers are untouched; no double count with Boxes (palletized boxes already leave the loose figure). `BLOCK_DELETE.Warehouse`. | **DEPLOYED LIVE** 2026-10-24 · commit pending | `index.js` (`/pal-line-status`, `/pal-topup`, `writeLineIntoBox`, `/pal-lines-box` + `/pal-line-box` SELECTs, `BLOCK_DELETE`), `palPlansApi.ts` (`warehouseId`, `setPalLineStatus(..., warehouse)`, `setPalLineLocation`), `PalletiseModal.tsx`, `DispatchBoard.tsx`, `LoadingBay.tsx`, `PalPlanDetail.tsx`, `Reports.tsx`, `ItemDetail.tsx`, `common/SetLocationModal.tsx`, `PanelOrderDetail.tsx` |
| 413 | **Panel Order form takes a Location.** Optional Location Combobox under Order Details (create / edit / clone), sent as `warehouse` on `/panel-order-save` (`""` clears; absent leaves it). No default — a new order has nothing on the floor yet; the detail's Set Location (CR-410) still works. | **DEPLOYED LIVE** 2026-10-24 · commit pending | `PanelOrderForm.tsx`, `panelOrdersApi.ts` (`PanelOrderInput.warehouse`), `index.js` (`/panel-order-save` header) |

Not done, on purpose: auto stock transfers on palletise / load (reverses the 2026-10-12 decision and multiplies
routes that would need counter-rows on split / release / discard); a `from_warehouse` on pal lines to steer the
loaded-box spread (matters only when ONE batch sits in several bays — the Loading-Bay-first / largest rule covers
the common case); a location on LoadBox (the container is the location). **Follow-up noted:** the client subtracts
palletized-not-loaded boxes from location on-hand (`batchStockApi` absorbed), the server (`serverWarehouseOnHand`)
subtracts only loaded lines — pre-existing, untouched here.

Deployed 2026-10-24 (`catalyst deploy`, client + both functions; smoke: app 200, api-health ok, data-ops booted).
Live data set up the same day (direct datastore writes, user's choice): bays **A1…F12 generated under all three
warehouses** (Production 69851000000366631 → A1 = 69851000000375797; Palletization 69851000000366632 → A1 =
69851000000373736; Loading Bay 69851000000366633 → A1 = 69851000000378670); the 9 production rows with no location
stamped onto Production ▸ A1; the Palletizing line 69851000000365087 placed at Palletization ▸ A1 and the Ready-for-Loading
line 69851000000362285 at Loading Bay ▸ A1 (the two Planning lines are loose stock — no location by design).
API drive `scripts/drive-cr411.mjs` (bulk-sheet + Record Output locations, Start / partial Complete / Set Location /
partial load on a pallet line, panel form location; ZZT, self-cleaning) is written — needs an Admin token
(`BOFFO_TOKEN=… node scripts/drive-cr411.mjs`), which the auto-mode session cannot mint.
Pending: run the drive, browser walk (sheet picker, Palletise dialog default, Set Location single + bulk, Pallets
segment), commit.

Find / Merge unchanged. Hardening: the two offset pagers (`lib/customerSync.js allRows`, `index.js zcqlAllRows`) add `ORDER BY ROWID` when the query has none — `LIMIT off, 300` without an order is an undefined page sequence (probed: ZCQL accepts `ORDER BY ROWID LIMIT 300, 3`); callers that already order are left alone. tsc + build, `customerDedupe.check` pass | Deployed 2026-10-22, awaiting user verify | `client/src/features/settings/ZohoSyncSettings.tsx` `onImport`, `functions/data-ops/lib/customerSync.js` `allRows`, `functions/data-ops/index.js` `zcqlAllRows` |

## CR-378 · BOFFO Glass design system — app-wide (2026-10-22)

| CR | Change | Status | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| 378 | **Our own "glass" design system, applied app-wide** (same day, second ask: "design across all pages apply the glass kit" — the three-area trial lasted one review; `main.tsx` now sets `data-skin="glass"` before the first paint and `GLASS_SECTIONS` is gone; a 46-route screenshot sweep in both themes added overrides for the production/loading sheets' sticky heads and footers, kanban columns and cards, and every tinted status chip that styles.css colours with literal dark text — `.chip.qstatus/.palstatus/.qc-verdict/.size/.finish/.brand`, `.qc-cell`, `.pallet-cell`, `.stage.teal`). First ask (user, 2026-10-22, with the Pane and Kairo reference screenshots + demo URL: "create a design system of our own using the same concept… Save this in the boffo. Reskin — customer and settings and home page only… Full glass… Dark, with light + system toggle"). The demos' production bundles were mined for the element inventory (plan file; no browser was available to click the live demo). **Mechanics:** `<html data-skin="glass">` is set by `App.tsx` (`GLASS_SECTIONS`, `useLayoutEffect`) only for `/dashboard`, `/parties*`, `/settings*`, `/glass-kit`; every rule in the new owner file `styles/glass.css` is prefixed with it, so the rest of the app keeps the slate/indigo theme untouched. `<html data-theme="dark\|light">` from `lib/theme.ts` (`localStorage["boffo.theme"]` dark \| light \| system, default dark, system follows the OS live, applied at module scope in `main.tsx` so there is no flash); header `ThemeToggle` (new `sun`/`moon`/`monitor` icons) cycles the three, shown on glass routes only. **System:** `--g-*` tokens per theme (page, text ×3, glass ×4, rim/depth/shadow, fill ×3, hairline, lens, accent ×3, 8 hues, rail, radii 28/22/16/12/pill, blur, ease, tint) + an override of the theme.css primaries so ~3 000 legacy rules re-theme by alias; wallpaper on `body::before`; floating dark rail (dark in BOTH themes — the logo needs a dark ground) with pill items + lens; transparent toolbar with a search pill and circle buttons; glass cards/KPI tiles/stage tiles (tint + rim + depth, **no blur** — blur only where content moves underneath: sticky `th`, floating grid footer, popovers, modals; never on `.card` or the fixed footer breaks); pill buttons with an accent **gel** `.primary`; fill inputs with a ring focus; new `.seg`/`.seg-btn`/`.badge`, `input.switch`, `.ident`/`.ini.hue-N`, `.av`, `.prio`, `.presence`; Kairo-style table (sentence-case 12/600 heads, 9×12 cells); glass settings workspace; opaque toasts (2026-07-20 mandate kept); `prefers-reduced-transparency` → solid, `prefers-reduced-motion` → no transitions. **Code:** `ui/Chip.tsx` tones moved from inline hex to `nd-chip tone-*` classes (`theme.css` keeps the identical hex + 9.4 % tint, zero change elsewhere; glass.css lightens them for dark); `ui/charts.tsx` passes `var(--c-…)`/`var(--accent-ink)` straight through (deleted `cssVar`, charts follow the toggle live); `Parties.tsx` toolbar moved **inside** the grid card, inline 26px button geometry removed, Customer column = initials tile + name / code, new **Status** column (Active/Inactive chip), segmented All / Active / Inactive quick filter with counts (`parties.seg`); `SettingsHome` "Allow duplicate batch numbers" = switch. **Kit:** `#/glass-kit` (Admin) renders every control and state. **Docs:** `docs/DESIGN-SYSTEM-GLASS.md` (portable spec). Print sheets (`.qprint-*`, `.qp-*`, `.dentry-*`) and the login screen keep their own palettes by design | Built (tsc + vite build pass), deploy pending | `client/src/styles/glass.css` (owner), `lib/theme.ts`, `main.tsx`, `App.tsx` `GLASS_SECTIONS`/`ThemeToggle`, `ui/Icon.tsx`, `ui/Chip.tsx` + `styles/theme.css` `.nd-chip.tone-*`, `ui/charts.tsx`, `features/masters/Parties.tsx`, `features/settings/SettingsHome.tsx`, `features/settings/GlassKit.tsx`, `docs/DESIGN-SYSTEM-GLASS.md` |

## CR-375 · Combobox chevron overlapped the text (2026-10-22)

|---|---|---|---|
| 375 | **Every Combobox's text ran under its chevron** (user, 2026-10-22, screenshot of the Loading sheet's Design cell: "the arrow always messes up with the name in the pick list"). Root cause, global: the house input skin `input:not([type]) { padding: 0 11px }` has specificity (0,1,1) and beat `.combo-input { padding-right: 28px }` (0,1,0), so the 28 px (46 px with the clear ×) reserved for the chevron never applied. Fix: the rule is `input.combo-input` (same specificity, later in the file) + `text-overflow: ellipsis`, so long labels ellipsise before the chevron. Client only, every Combobox in the app. | **LIVE 2026-10-22** (client only) — `tsc` + `vite build` pass; commit pending | [`styles.css`](../client/src/styles/styles.css) `input.combo-input` |

## CR-372 · The customer sync never creates a twin — duplicates are an error, not a row (2026-10-22)

If any customer exists, and it comes from books as well. It is just mapped to that based on the name. CRM - Books - Addon"). `customerSync.run` read the existing customers with an unbounded ZCQL — **capped at 300 rows** (CR-339) — so past 300 customers every import page saw an arbitrary slice: `byLink` missed the account → created again with the same `crm_account_id`, and `nextCustomerCode` over the slice minted codes twice. Fix: the read is paged (`allRows`, LIMIT off,300). Matching now: link column → **one** unmapped customer with the same name is linked (CRM → Books → add-on: the existing Books/add-on customer is adopted by the CRM account, CRM values win non-empty) → else create. **Duplicate = no write**: the name already on a customer linked to another CRM account ("duplicate — already linked to CUS-… (Zoho CRM id); merge the accounts in Zoho CRM") or on several unmapped customers ("N customers with this name here — merge them … first") goes to `summary.errors` + `summary.duplicates`, and the watermark still advances (a duplicate is a decision, not a transient failure — the old `break` would have retried the same record forever). Settings pane: "· N duplicates" on the last-run line, Note wording says duplicates stay skipped | Built, deploy pending | `functions/data-ops/lib/customerSync.js`, `client/src/features/masters/zohoApi.ts` `SyncSummary.duplicates`, `settings/ZohoSyncSettings.tsx` |

Not the data: `lib/zoho.js` kept ONE cached access token, so every CRM → Books → CRM switch refreshed again (~2 refreshes per account); Zoho allows **10 access-token requests per 10 minutes per app** ([token limits](https://www.zoho.com/accounts/protocol/oauth/token-limits.html)) → "Access Denied / too many requests" → `accessTokenFor` said 409 reauth → `booksContactFor` swallowed it and the rest of the page became "CRM only" (LIVE: 22 Books links in two bursts of 11, nothing between; the very first import request died with 409 — the CRM refresh itself throttled). **Fix:** `tokenCache` is a Map keyed by refresh token (each provider refreshes once an hour); a throttled refresh is a **429 "wait 10 minutes"**, never a reconnect prompt. **Visible:** any Books failure stops the checks for the run and lands in the summary errors once ("Zoho Books check stopped: …"). **Re-check:** the import hands back the hits it skips as unchanged (`pickChanged` → `unchanged`), and the adapter's new `recheck(ctx, aux, row)` does a Books-only lookup for rows with a CRM id and no Books id (700 ms gap — Books allows 100 calls/min), writes `books_contact_id`, counts as `linked`, OperationLog `crm-sync:recheck`; `existing` selects both link columns; one `aux` per run. So **Import all is the re-check** — no new route or button. | BUILT 2026-10-21 — `booksSync.check.js` (unchanged list) + `crmSync.check.js` + `tsc` + `vite build` pass; deploy, Admin re-runs Import all, commit pending | [`zoho.js`](../functions/data-ops/lib/zoho.js) `tokenCache` / `accessTokenFor`; [`crmSync.js`](../functions/data-ops/lib/crmSync.js) `booksContactFor` / `recheck`; [`customerSync.js`](../functions/data-ops/lib/customerSync.js) `pickChanged` / re-check loop |

## CR-362 · Customer Zoho link status — CRM & Books / CRM only / Books only (2026-10-21)

| CR | Change | Status | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| 362 | **Every customer shows which Zoho systems hold it** (user, 2026-10-21, Gujarati: "Zoho Books and Zoho CRM customers are synced. Both link ids already sat on the row (`crm_account_id` CR-359, `books_contact_id` CR-356 — hidden, values kept) but nothing tied them: since CR-359 the Books id was never written for CRM-pulled customers, so a badge alone would have read "CRM only" for everyone. **Server:** the CRM adapter's `fetch` adds ONE Books call, `GET /books/v3/contacts?zcrm_account_id={account id}` — Zoho's own CRM ⇄ Books integration stamps that id on the Books contact, so a hit is the real link (not our name matching); `loadAux` carries the Books connection (null = not connected = skipped), a Books error never fails the CRM record (409 stops asking for the rest of the run), and `accountToCustomer` writes `books_contact_id` only when there is a hit (`// ponytail:` never clears a stale link). No schema change. LIVE check before building: Books AND CRM connected (org Boffo Granito LLP), no CRM watermark yet → the pending first Import all fills every customer. | **LIVE 2026-10-21** (two deploys: feature, then the import-page fix) — `crmSync.check.js` (2 asserts) + `booksSync.check.js` (skip rule) + `tsc` + `vite build` pass; first Import all brought in ~560 CRM accounts (22 with a Books link) before the page timeout; Admin re-runs Import all to finish; **22 of ~560 Books links only → CR-363** (token-refresh throttle, fixed same day); commit pending | [`crmSync.js`](../functions/data-ops/lib/crmSync.js) `booksContactFor` / `loadAux` / `fetch`; [`zohoApi.ts`](../client/src/features/masters/zohoApi.ts) `zohoLinkOf` / `ZOHO_LINK`; [`customersApi.ts`](../client/src/features/masters/customersApi.ts) `zohoLink`; [`Parties.tsx`](../client/src/features/masters/Parties.tsx); [`CustomerDetail.tsx`](../client/src/features/masters/CustomerDetail.tsx) |

## CR-358 · Books → add-on sync fires on Catalyst Signals; cron demoted to daily backstop (2026-10-21) — ⚠ Books hidden by CR-359 (route answers `disabled:true`; code kept)

| CR | Change | Status | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| 358 | **Zoho Books customer changes reach the add-on the moment they happen** (user, 2026-10-21: "Zoho Catalyst has signal. It fetches the data immediately after action. If this works, we might need to drop the cron job… Books to add on sync"). Verified in the Catalyst docs: Signals has a **Zoho Books publisher** with `customer_created` / `customer_updated` events and a **Webhook** target (static query params, GET, Instant dispatch, 1–300 calls/s, retries, 24 h retention). Design = reuse: the webhook target is the **existing** `GET /cron/books-sync?key=` route — the watermark pull already knows what changed, so the (undocumented) event payload is ignored and no new function/route exists. **Console (Admin):** publisher `boffo_books` (Zoho Books → the Boffo org — the console login must be a member of that org; Event Ordering on; Authorize), webhook target `GET …/server/data-ops/cron/books-sync` with `key` = `FX_CRON_KEY`, rate limit 1/s, rules on `customer_created` + `customer_updated`, dispatch Instant (default batching), 3 retries; cron `books_sync_30m` → **daily** `books_sync_daily` (decision 2026-10-21: keep as backstop, one list call a day — covers a lapsed authorization or an event dropped after 24 h). **Code:** `syncFromBooks` runs one at a time per instance (`inflight` lock — webhook + cron + Refresh now + Import landing seconds apart could both insert the same new contact); comments and the three Settings ▸ Zoho Books strings say "as they change" instead of "every 30 minutes". Known echo: a Push from the add-on fires `customer_updated` → one extra pull of that contact, harmless (Books wins with the values just sent). No delete event exists in Books — same as before. | BUILT 2026-10-21 — `booksSync.check.js` (new serialization assertion) passes, build clean; **deploy + console setup pending**: deploy `data-ops` + client, then Admin creates the publisher/target/rules and reschedules the cron; verify by editing a customer in Books → `books-sync:update` OperationLog row within seconds | [`booksSync.js`](../functions/data-ops/lib/booksSync.js) `inflight`, [`booksSync.check.js`](../functions/data-ops/lib/booksSync.check.js), [`index.js`](../functions/data-ops/index.js) `/cron/books-sync`, [`ZohoBooksSettings.tsx`](../client/src/features/settings/ZohoBooksSettings.tsx) |

| CR | Change | Status | Where |
|---|---|---|---|

New customer can be added in the addon and synced manually and refresh time of 30 minutes. Do not do all records pull to save api calls."; decisions 2026-10-21: Books → add-on every 30 min, add-on → Books by button, one-time Import all, **Books wins**, eight fields). **Reverses the CR-325 rule "never an auto sync".** (1) **Books → add-on, every 30 min** — `GET /cron/books-sync?key=` (same `FX_CRON_KEY`, pre-guard; Catalyst URL cron `books_sync_30m`) and Settings ▸ Zoho Books ▸ **Refresh now** run `syncFromBooks` (`lib/booksSync.js`): one `GET /contacts` page sorted `last_modified_time` desc, stop at the watermark `AppSetting zoho_books_sync_at`, then `GET /contacts/:id` per changed contact (oldest first, 50 per run), matched by `books_contact_id` → else by name (case-insensitive, unmapped only — **links** it) → else created with the next `CUS-` code; a Books value overwrites only where non-empty, add-on-only columns untouched, `status=inactive` → Active No; every write → OperationLog actor `zoho-books-sync`; no watermark = nothing imported yet = the refresh does nothing and says so. (2) **Import all customers** (Admin, `POST /zoho/books/import {page}`, 25 per call, client loops until `done`) is the only full pull — the two existing customers link by name instead of duplicating. (3) **Add-on → Books by hand** — customer More ▸ **Create in Zoho Books** / **Push to Zoho Books** (`POST /zoho/books/push/:rowid`, `customerToContact`, POST or PUT `/contacts`, stores the new `contact_id`); needs the write scope, so `SCOPE_BOOKS` adds `contacts.CREATE/UPDATE`, the granted scope is stored (`zoho_books_scope`), status reports `canWrite`, the Settings card shows **Reconnect** and the push item says why it is disabled. (4) **Eight Customer columns** = Books contact fields: `gst_no`, `gst_treatment`, `place_of_contact`, `pan_no`, `website`, `credit_limit`, `notes`, `opening_balance` (display-only, never pushed) — **Tax & Other** block on the customer form, rows on the detail, mapped in `booksMap.ts` (form prefill) and its server twin `booksSync.js` (pull); `booksCall` generalises `booksGet` to POST/PUT. (5) **Customers grid** gets a **Zoho Books** column (Synced chip / dash, after Payment Term, default visible) and a Synced / Not synced advanced-search filter (same-day follow-up: "a column for hint that it is synced with zoho"). (6) Two guard fixes found while mapping: non-Admins can no longer write any `zoho_books_*` AppSetting row through the generic routes, and a `?columns=` projection on AppSetting can no longer hide `setting_key` from the secret-row filter. **API console: nothing to change** — scopes are granted at consent; the Admin reconnects once. | BUILT 2026-10-21 — tsc + build clean, `booksSync.check.js` + `booksMap.check.ts` pass; 8 Customer columns added 2026-10-21 via the Catalyst MCP (ids 69851000000347446…453); **LIVE 2026-10-21** — `/cron/books-sync` probed (403 on a wrong key, 200 "Run Import all first" on the right one), cron `books_sync_30m` created (id 69851000000347464); **Admin still to do:** Reconnect in Settings ▸ Zoho Books, then Import all customers; commit pending | [`booksSync.js`](../functions/data-ops/lib/booksSync.js), [`zoho.js`](../functions/data-ops/lib/zoho.js) `booksCall` / `canWriteBooks` / routes, [`index.js`](../functions/data-ops/index.js) `/cron/books-sync` + `assertSettingWrite`, [`booksMap.ts`](../client/src/features/masters/booksMap.ts), [`booksApi.ts`](../client/src/features/masters/booksApi.ts), [`customersApi.ts`](../client/src/features/masters/customersApi.ts) `GST_TREATMENTS`, [`PartyForm.tsx`](../client/src/features/masters/PartyForm.tsx) Tax & Other, [`CustomerDetail.tsx`](../client/src/features/masters/CustomerDetail.tsx) push, [`ZohoBooksSettings.tsx`](../client/src/features/settings/ZohoBooksSettings.tsx) |

## CR-324 · Sign in with Zoho (2026-10-12)

| CR | Change | Status | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| 324 | **The login page gains "Sign in with Zoho" on top; the password form stays below as the fallback** (user: "all our users have Zoho credentials; user with Zoho mail will get access"). Zoho OAuth authorization-code flow ported from the SKU generator, identity only (`AaaServer.profile.READ`, online, nothing stored): `POST /auth/zoho/start` sets a 10-min `boffo_oauth_state` cookie and returns the consent URL on `accounts.zoho.in`; Zoho returns to the SPA `/app/?code=…&state=…&location=…`; `AuthGate` posts to `/auth/zoho/exchange`, which checks the state, exchanges on the callback's DC, reads the profile and **mints the same session as `/auth/login`** (`mintSession`) — only for an email that is an active `AppUser`; anyone else gets "No BOFFO account for …". Needs a Server-based client in the Zoho API console (redirect URI = the SPA `/app/`, trailing slash) and `ZOHO_CLIENT_ID/SECRET/REDIRECT_URI/DC` in `catalyst-config.json`, which is now gitignored (`catalyst-config.example.json` committed). | BUILT 2026-10-12 (tsc + build, offline route smoke test, booksMap self-check); deploy + browser drive pending the Zoho API-console client; commit pending | [`appauth.js`](../functions/data-ops/lib/appauth.js) `mintSession`, `/auth/zoho/*`, [`zoho.js`](../functions/data-ops/lib/zoho.js), [`AuthGate.tsx`](../client/src/AuthGate.tsx), [`auth.ts`](../client/src/lib/auth.ts), `login.css`, `.gitignore` |

## CR-312 · Cut-piece booking per panel order (allocate / release; Dispatch = fully booked) (2026-10-07)

| CR | Change | Status | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| 312 | **Cut pieces are BOOKED to a panel order (`PanelOrder.allocation` JSON `{design\|cutSize: pcs}`, a claim on on-hand — never a stock movement, the SO `alloc` idea): free = on hand − Σ every open order's booking; an order's Short = need − booked − free (what must still be cut for it). Requirements card gains Allocated · Short columns + an "Allocate Stock" button (always visible for editors, greyed with the reason) opening ONE modal that sets the booked figure per row in both directions (higher = book free pieces, lower = release, 0 = none; reallocate = release here, book on the other order). Dispatch now requires every piece booked (server 409 "Book N more pcs of … before dispatch", button greyed with the same text) — that is what stops order A dispatching pieces booked to order B. Ready books its own cutting output (allocation = need) so the straight path needs no extra click; a direct Received → Dispatch books first.** (spec §7–8; user decisions 2026-10-07: keep the per-row Available tick beside Allocated; Dispatch requires full booking.) Server: `POST /panel-order-allocate/:rowid {allocation}` (open order only; per key integer 0…need and ≤ on hand − `otherAllocations`; verify all, one write; `logRelated`), `/panel-order-status` (Dispatched gate + Ready auto-book), `/panel-order-save` clamps the booking to the new need on edit, `/cut-stock-entry` refuses a removal below Σ open bookings (409 names the booked count), `/migrate-panel-orders` books already-Ready orders; `allocation` in `STATE_COLUMNS`.
